  "meta_commentary": "This plan threads a new `timeline_placement` contract through 4 layers: frontend selection \u2192 agent tool \u2192 task resolver persistence \u2192 completion-time insertion. The critical persistence boundary is in the `create-task` resolvers \u2014 `buildTaskParams()` in `imageGeneration.ts` uses an explicit whitelist that will silently drop unknown fields. The plan addresses this via `setTaskLineageFields()` in `lineage.ts`, which already has precedent in `magicEdit.ts` and `zImageTurboI2I.ts`.\n\nExecution ordering matters: land the contract + lineage extension first (T1-T2), then wire the agent tool layer (T3-T4), then complete_task consumption (T5-T6), then tests (T7-T9). The prompt updates in T4 should be conservative \u2014 the model needs to know the schema field exists but shouldn't over-specify placement for every request.\n\nKey gotcha: `imageGeneration.ts` does NOT currently call `setTaskLineageFields` at all, unlike magicEdit and zImageTurboI2I. The executor must add that call after `buildTaskParams` returns, matching the pattern in the other resolvers. `buildQueuedTask()` passes params through without filtering, so once lineage sets the field, it survives to the task row.\n\nFor `complete_task`, the plan reuses existing timeline mutation helpers from `tools/timeline.ts` and versioned save from `db.ts`. The executor should import `addMediaClip()` and `saveTimelineConfigVersioned()` rather than writing a new insertion path. If the timeline mutation fails after generation creation, the task should still complete \u2014 log the placement failure as a follow-up issue, don't lose the output asset.\n\nThe frontend changes (Step 1) are the least risky \u2014 they extend existing types and add fields to the attachment mapping in AgentChat.tsx.     "PERSISTENCE BOUNDARY: imageGeneration.ts buildTaskParams() uses an explicit whitelist. timeline_placement must be injected via setTaskLineageFields AFTER buildTaskParams returns, not inside it. Verify buildQueuedTask passes enriched params through without filtering.",
    "RESOLVER GAP: imageGeneration.ts currently does NOT call setTaskLineageFields at all \u2014 this is the only edit-family resolver missing the call. magicEdit.ts and zImageTurboI2I.ts already have it. The executor must add it, not assume it exists.",
    "COMPLETE_TASK RESILIENCE: If timeline mutation fails after generation creation succeeds, the task must still complete. Do not wrap both in a single try/catch that would lose the output asset. Match existing pattern of preserving primary completion.",
    "TIMELINE STATE LOADING: The agent loop already loads timeline state at loop.ts:419. Selection enrichment should use this already-loaded state, not fetch it again.",
    "VERSIONED SAVE: Timeline insertion in complete_task must use saveTimelineConfigVersioned (optimistic concurrency) from db.ts, not a raw update. Read current config_version before mutation.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-tool-utils: agent-tool-utils: step 6 moves `isrecord` out of `llm/messages.ts` without preserving that module's current export surface for `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, so the refactor plan can break four existing imports unless it adds a re-export or rewires those callers. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual export surface in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts` and its consumers. step 6 says to 'remove local `isrecord`' from `messages.ts` and import it from `utils.ts`, but without also preserving `messages.ts` as an exporter or rewiring `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, that change would break the module graph immediately. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the new `useaudioparam()` sketch against `src/tools/video-editor/effects/compileeffect.tsx:1-2`. the plan defines it in terms of `interpolate(audio[binding.source], ...)` but does not explicitly import `interpolate` into the new hook file or simplify it to straight arithmetic, so the helper's implementation contract is still looser than it needs to be. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked revised step 2.4 against `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useclipresize.ts:76-100`. the plan still says to clamp audio by writing back to `action.end`, but the current hook treats the callback `action` as input data and then creates fresh row actions from it. mutating that caller-owned object is unnecessary and risks coupling the resize hook to outside references instead of using a local `clampedend` variable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the hand-built replacement clip against the existing insert path in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:201-252,262-271` and serialization in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts:210-216`. the revised `timelineeditor` sketch still bypasses current insertion invariants such as manual-track geometry defaults and explicit `cliporder` updates, so replacement clips on manual visual tracks will not be initialized the same way as other inserted video clips and may serialize in a different per-track order. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` design is still underspecified against `materializedata()`. `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so if the executor literally follows step 2.4 and overwrites `pinnedshotgroups` on an already-materialized `timelinedata` before `commitdata()`, the saved config and the cached `stablesignature` will diverge unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the revised grouped-drag plan fixes the pointer-down selection regression, but it still leaves `pinnedshotgroup.trackid` stale on cross-track moves. the data model stores `trackid`, and current drag/move helpers only update rows/meta/clip order, so a pinned group dragged to another track would keep pointing at the old track unless the drag commit also patches group metadata. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked step 2.4 against `materializedata()` in `usetimelinecommit` and `assembletimelinedata()` in `timeline-data.ts`. the plan still says to overwrite `pinnedshotgroups` after `materializedata()` returns, but `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so mutating the config afterward would leave signature-derived state stale unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the grouped-drag plan against `useclipdrag` and `usetimelinetrackmanagement.movecliptorow()`. the plan fixes the pointer-down selection regression, but it still does not say how `pinnedshotgroup.trackid` is updated when a grouped drag or move lands on a different row, so the overlay and menu state can still point at the old track after a successful cross-track move. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the cross-track multi-drag new-track branch in `useclipdrag`. that path still commits through `applyedit({ type: 'config', resolvedconfig })` using `buildconfigfromdragresult()`, and the revised plan does not explain how pinned-group metadata is updated atomically on that path, so grouped drags that create a new track are still under-specified even beyond the stale-`trackid` problem.   "meta_commentary": "This plan threads a new `timeline_placement` contract through 4 layers: frontend selection \u2192 agent tool \u2192 task resolver persistence \u2192 completion-time insertion. The critical persistence boundary is in the `create-task` resolvers \u2014 `buildTaskParams()` in `imageGeneration.ts` uses an explicit whitelist that will silently drop unknown fields. The plan addresses this via `setTaskLineageFields()` in `lineage.ts`, which already has precedent in `magicEdit.ts` and `zImageTurboI2I.ts`.\n\nExecution ordering matters: land the contract + lineage extension first (T1-T2), then wire the agent tool layer (T3-T4), then complete_task consumption (T5-T6), then tests (T7-T9). The prompt updates in T4 should be conservative \u2014 the model needs to know the schema field exists but shouldn't over-specify placement for every request.\n\nKey gotcha: `imageGeneration.ts` does NOT currently call `setTaskLineageFields` at all, unlike magicEdit and zImageTurboI2I. The executor must add that call after `buildTaskParams` returns, matching the pattern in the other resolvers. `buildQueuedTask()` passes params through without filtering, so once lineage sets the field, it survives to the task row.\n\nFor `complete_task`, the plan reuses existing timeline mutation helpers from `tools/timeline.ts` and versioned save from `db.ts`. The executor should import `addMediaClip()` and `saveTimelineConfigVersioned()` rather than writing a new insertion path. If the timeline mutation fails after generation creation, the task should still complete \u2014 log the placement failure as a follow-up issue, don't lose the output asset.\n\nThe frontend changes (Step 1) are the least risky \u2014 they extend existing types and add fields to the attachment mapping in AgentChat.tsx.     "PERSISTENCE BOUNDARY: imageGeneration.ts buildTaskParams() uses an explicit whitelist. timeline_placement must be injected via setTaskLineageFields AFTER buildTaskParams returns, not inside it. Verify buildQueuedTask passes enriched params through without filtering.",
    "RESOLVER GAP: imageGeneration.ts currently does NOT call setTaskLineageFields at all \u2014 this is the only edit-family resolver missing the call. magicEdit.ts and zImageTurboI2I.ts already have it. The executor must add it, not assume it exists.",
    "COMPLETE_TASK RESILIENCE: If timeline mutation fails after generation creation succeeds, the task must still complete. Do not wrap both in a single try/catch that would lose the output asset. Match existing pattern of preserving primary completion.",
    "TIMELINE STATE LOADING: The agent loop already loads timeline state at loop.ts:419. Selection enrichment should use this already-loaded state, not fetch it again.",
    "VERSIONED SAVE: Timeline insertion in complete_task must use saveTimelineConfigVersioned (optimistic concurrency) from db.ts, not a raw update. Read current config_version before mutation.",
    "REUSE EXISTING HELPERS: Timeline clip insertion must use the existing addMediaClip() from tools/timeline.ts, not a new ad-hoc write path. This is a 'should' success criterion.",

Checkpoint written to `.megaplan/plans/light-megaplan-for-timeline-20260410-0114/execution_batch_4.json`.","files_changed":["supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.ts","supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.test.ts","supabase/functions/complete_task/handler.test.ts","supabase/functions/_tests/harness/cases.ts",".megaplan/plans/light-megaplan-for-timeline-20260410-0114/execution_batch_4.json"],"commands_run":["npx vitest run --config config/testing/vitest.edge.config.ts supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.test.ts","npx vitest run --config config/testing/vitest.edge.config.ts supabase/functions/complete_task/handler.test.ts","HARNESS_ENABLED=1 npx vitest run --config config/testing/vitest.edge.smoke.config.ts supabase/functions/_tests/harness/harness.test.ts"],"deviations":["T8 required one supporting implementation tweak beyond the planned file list: `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.ts` now conditionally includes `timeline_placement` only when the agent supplied one, matching the new omission-path assertion.","The standard edge Vitest config excludes `supabase/functions/_tests/**/*.test.ts`, so harness verification was rerun with `config/testing/vitest.edge.smoke.config.ts` after the first edge-config attempt matched no harness test files."],"task_updates":[{"task_id":"T8","status":"done","executor_notes":"Added create-task tests that assert `executeCreateTask()` forwards `timeline_placement` when present and omits it when absent, added complete_task handler tests that verify timeline insertion is attempted for stored placement intent and that completion still succeeds with a degraded follow-up when insertion fails, and added a harness case that selects a timeline-backed clip and expects task params to include `timeline_placement` plus downstream generation and timeline insertion effects. Verification: full `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.test.ts`, `supabase/functions/complete_task/handler.test.ts`, and smoke `supabase/functions/_tests/harness/harness.test.ts` passed.","files_changed":["supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.ts","supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.test.ts","supabase/functions/complete_task/handler.test.ts","supabase/functions/_tests/harness/cases.ts"],"commands_run":["npx vitest run --config config/testing/vitest.edge.config.ts supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.test.ts","npx vitest run --config config/testing/vitest.edge.config.ts supabase/functions/complete_task/handler.test.ts","HARNESS_ENABLED=1 npx vitest run --config config/testing/vitest.edge.smoke.config.ts supabase/functions/_tests/harness/harness.test.ts"]}],"sense_check_acknowledgments":[{"sense_check_id":"SC8","executor_note":"Added a harness case that selects a timeline-backed clip, leaves `skipTaskCompletion` unset so the runner waits for async task side effects, and asserts the resulting task params contain `timeline_placement`, a generation is created, and a media clip is added on the source track after completion."}]}

  "meta_commentary": "This plan threads a new `timeline_placement` contract through 4 layers: frontend selection \u2192 agent tool \u2192 task resolver persistence \u2192 completion-time insertion. The critical persistence boundary is in the `create-task` resolvers \u2014 `buildTaskParams()` in `imageGeneration.ts` uses an explicit whitelist that will silently drop unknown fields. The plan addresses this via `setTaskLineageFields()` in `lineage.ts`, which already has precedent in `magicEdit.ts` and `zImageTurboI2I.ts`.\n\nExecution ordering matters: land the contract + lineage extension first (T1-T2), then wire the agent tool layer (T3-T4), then complete_task consumption (T5-T6), then tests (T7-T9). The prompt updates in T4 should be conservative \u2014 the model needs to know the schema field exists but shouldn't over-specify placement for every request.\n\nKey gotcha: `imageGeneration.ts` does NOT currently call `setTaskLineageFields` at all, unlike magicEdit and zImageTurboI2I. The executor must add that call after `buildTaskParams` returns, matching the pattern in the other resolvers. `buildQueuedTask()` passes params through without filtering, so once lineage sets the field, it survives to the task row.\n\nFor `complete_task`, the plan reuses existing timeline mutation helpers from `tools/timeline.ts` and versioned save from `db.ts`. The executor should import `addMediaClip()` and `saveTimelineConfigVersioned()` rather than writing a new insertion path. If the timeline mutation fails after generation creation, the task should still complete \u2014 log the placement failure as a follow-up issue, don't lose the output asset.\n\nThe frontend changes (Step 1) are the least risky \u2014 they extend existing types and add fields to the attachment mapping in AgentChat.tsx. The server-side enrichment in selectedClips.ts needs timeline state that's already loaded in the agent loop.",

    "PERSISTENCE BOUNDARY: imageGeneration.ts buildTaskParams() uses an explicit whitelist. timeline_placement must be injected via setTaskLineageFields AFTER buildTaskParams returns, not inside it. Verify buildQueuedTask passes enriched params through without filtering.",

    "COMPLETE_TASK RESILIENCE: If timeline mutation fails after generation creation succeeds, the task must still complete. Do not wrap both in a single try/catch that would lose the output asset. Match existing pattern of preserving primary completion.",
    "TIMELINE STATE LOADING: The agent loop already loads timeline state at loop.ts:419. Selection enrichment should use this already-loaded state, not fetch it again.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-tool-utils: agent-tool-utils: step 6 moves `isrecord` out of `llm/messages.ts` without preserving that module's current export surface for `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, so the refactor plan can break four existing imports unless it adds a re-export or rewires those callers. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual export surface in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts` and its consumers. step 6 says to 'remove local `isrecord`' from `messages.ts` and import it from `utils.ts`, but without also preserving `messages.ts` as an exporter or rewiring `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, that change would break the module graph immediately. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the new `useaudioparam()` sketch against `src/tools/video-editor/effects/compileeffect.tsx:1-2`. the plan defines it in terms of `interpolate(audio[binding.source], ...)` but does not explicitly import `interpolate` into the new hook file or simplify it to straight arithmetic, so the helper's implementation contract is still looser than it needs to be. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked revised step 2.4 against `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useclipresize.ts:76-100`. the plan still says to clamp audio by writing back to `action.end`, but the current hook treats the callback `action` as input data and then creates fresh row actions from it. mutating that caller-owned object is unnecessary and risks coupling the resize hook to outside references instead of using a local `clampedend` variable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the hand-built replacement clip against the existing insert path in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:201-252,262-271` and serialization in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts:210-216`. the revised `timelineeditor` sketch still bypasses current insertion invariants such as manual-track geometry defaults and explicit `cliporder` updates, so replacement clips on manual visual tracks will not be initialized the same way as other inserted video clips and may serialize in a different per-track order. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` design is still underspecified against `materializedata()`. `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so if the executor literally follows step 2.4 and overwrites `pinnedshotgroups` on an already-materialized `timelinedata` before `commitdata()`, the saved config and the cached `stablesignature` will diverge unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the revised grouped-drag plan fixes the pointer-down selection regression, but it still leaves `pinnedshotgroup.trackid` stale on cross-track moves. the data model stores `trackid`, and current drag/move helpers only update rows/meta/clip order, so a pinned group dragged to another track would keep pointing at the old track unless the drag commit also patches group metadata. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked step 2.4 against `materializedata()` in `usetimelinecommit` and `assembletimelinedata()` in `timeline-data.ts`. the plan still says to overwrite `pinnedshotgroups` after `materializedata()` returns, but `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so mutating the config afterward would leave signature-derived state stale unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the grouped-drag plan against `useclipdrag` and `usetimelinetrackmanagement.movecliptorow()`. the plan fixes the pointer-down selection regression, but it still does not say how `pinnedshotgroup.trackid` is updated when a grouped drag or move lands on a different row, so the overlay and menu state can still point at the old track after a successful cross-track move. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the cross-track multi-drag new-track branch in `useclipdrag`. that path still commits through `applyedit({ type: 'config', resolvedconfig })` using `buildconfigfromdragresult()`, and the revised plan does not explain how pinned-group metadata is updated atomically on that path, so grouped drags that create a new track are still under-specified even beyond the stale-`trackid` problem. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the proposed shared interaction-state location against the actual hook graph in `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelineeditor.tsx:194`, `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelineeditor.tsx:479`, `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinestate.ts:172-204`, and `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinestate.contexts.ts:72-166`. rev 3 says the ref should live in `usetimelinestate.contexts.ts`, but that file only memoizes context values while `useclipdrag`, `timelinecanvas`, and `usepinnedgroupsync` are all coordinated from `timelineeditor`, so the stated location and rationale are still technically off.   "meta_commentary": "This plan threads a new `timeline_placement` contract through 4 layers: frontend selection \u2192 agent tool \u2192 task resolver persistence \u2192 completion-time insertion. The critical persistence boundary is in the `create-task` resolvers \u2014 `buildTaskParams()` in `imageGeneration.ts` uses an explicit whitelist that will silently drop unknown fields. The plan addresses this via `setTaskLineageFields()` in `lineage.ts`, which already has precedent in `magicEdit.ts` and `zImageTurboI2I.ts`.\n\nExecution ordering matters: land the contract + lineage extension first (T1-T2), then wire the agent tool layer (T3-T4), then complete_task consumption (T5-T6), then tests (T7-T9). The prompt updates in T4 should be conservative \u2014 the model needs to know the schema field exists but shouldn't over-specify placement for every request.\n\nKey gotcha: `imageGeneration.ts` does NOT currently call `setTaskLineageFields` at all, unlike magicEdit and zImageTurboI2I. The executor must add that call after `buildTaskParams` returns, matching the pattern in the other resolvers. `buildQueuedTask()` passes params through without filtering, so once lineage sets the field, it survives to the task row.\n\nFor `complete_task`, the plan reuses existing timeline mutation helpers from `tools/timeline.ts` and versioned save from `db.ts`. The executor should import `addMediaClip()` and `saveTimelineConfigVersioned()` rather than writing a new insertion path. If the timeline mutation fails after generation creation, the task should still complete \u2014 log the placement failure as a follow-up issue, don't lose the output asset.\n\nThe frontend changes (Step 1) are the least risky \u2014 they extend existing types and add fields to the attachment mapping in AgentChat.tsx. The server-side enrichment in selectedClips.ts needs timeline state that's already loaded in the agent loop.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-tool-utils: agent-tool-utils: step 6 moves `isrecord` out of `llm/messages.ts` without preserving that module's current export surface for `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, so the refactor plan can break four existing imports unless it adds a re-export or rewires those callers. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual export surface in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts` and its consumers. step 6 says to 'remove local `isrecord`' from `messages.ts` and import it from `utils.ts`, but without also preserving `messages.ts` as an exporter or rewiring `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, that change would break the module graph immediately. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the new `useaudioparam()` sketch against `src/tools/video-editor/effects/compileeffect.tsx:1-2`. the plan defines it in terms of `interpolate(audio[binding.source], ...)` but does not explicitly import `interpolate` into the new hook file or simplify it to straight arithmetic, so the helper's implementation contract is still looser than it needs to be. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked revised step 2.4 against `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useclipresize.ts:76-100`. the plan still says to clamp audio by writing back to `action.end`, but the current hook treats the callback `action` as input data and then creates fresh row actions from it. mutating that caller-owned object is unnecessary and risks coupling the resize hook to outside references instead of using a local `clampedend` variable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the hand-built replacement clip against the existing insert path in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:201-252,262-271` and serialization in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts:210-216`. the revised `timelineeditor` sketch still bypasses current insertion invariants such as manual-track geometry defaults and explicit `cliporder` updates, so replacement clips on manual visual tracks will not be initialized the same way as other inserted video clips and may serialize in a different per-track order. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` design is still underspecified against `materializedata()`. `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so if the executor literally follows step 2.4 and overwrites `pinnedshotgroups` on an already-materialized `timelinedata` before `commitdata()`, the saved config and the cached `stablesignature` will diverge unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the revised grouped-drag plan fixes the pointer-down selection regression, but it still leaves `pinnedshotgroup.trackid` stale on cross-track moves. the data model stores `trackid`, and current drag/move helpers only update rows/meta/clip order, so a pinned group dragged to another track would keep pointing at the old track unless the drag commit also patches group metadata. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked step 2.4 against `materializedata()` in `usetimelinecommit` and `assembletimelinedata()` in `timeline-data.ts`. the plan still says to overwrite `pinnedshotgroups` after `materializedata()` returns, but `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so mutating the config afterward would leave signature-derived state stale unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

(flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
